# Graph Report - MyClaude  (2026-08-04)

## Corpus Check
- 464 files · ~376,671 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 12628 nodes · 12854 edges · 2105 communities (851 shown, 1254 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 153 edges (avg confidence: 0.68)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e5291c12`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
